> While WO-2026-084 successfully aligned advocate perspectives to the active batting/pitching teams, it completely omitted game location/venue awareness. The M.A.R.D. engine does not supply active venue telemetry (e.g. home_team, away_team, venue_name, venue_location) into the system prompts of the seated advocates. As a result, the LLM is blindly pattern-matching off the fact that "Wheeler is on the mound" and defaults to home-pride rhetoric, without checking if the active venue is actually the opponent's stadium.
> 
>

>       * Directive: "You are the HOME team. The game is being played at YOUR home stadium, {game.venue_name} in {game.venue_location}. Defend your house, hype up the home crowd, and let the visiting opponents know they are in hostile territory."

>       * Directive: "You are the AWAY team. The game is being played on the road at the opponent's home stadium, {game.venue_name} in {game.venue_location}. You are a visiting antagonist. Your goal is to silence their home crowd, mock their stadium layout, and express that you are here to take over their house."
>    * If both are False: Default to standard neutral spectator guidelines.

Because game.home_team = "NYM" and game.away_team = "PHI" during tonight's game, the compiler must explicitly block him from using any "home-field" or "our house" strings and force him into a visiting antagonist role.

#### 📋 STRY-0626-LOCAL-VISION-AI: WO-2026-093 Local Vision-Driven Interface Training Framework and AI Knowledge Base

> 1. Ingested AI Articles Knowledge Base
> * KDnuggets (Top 7 Coding Models in 2026): Local 24B+ parameter models running on mid-tier consumer GPUs are achieving rapid coding execution speeds by leveraging Multi-Token Prediction (MTP), Quantization-Aware Training (QAT), and sparse Mixture of Experts (MoE) architectures. Optimized models like Qwen3.6 27B MTP and Gemma 4 31B IT QAT will serve as local fallback coding nodes.
> * Equinix (Hybrid Multicloud Networking): Bypasses the latency, packet jitter, and vendor egress fees of the public internet by using dedicated Layer 2 private connections (Equinix Fabric) inside neutral datacenters to synchronize state between cloud nodes.

> * Machine Learning Mastery (AI Browser Agents): Transitions automation from fragile HTML DOM-parsing (Selenium/Playwright) to visual, intent-driven browser navigation using Multimodal AI vision models and the Set-of-Mark (SOM) technique.
> 2. Pi 5 + Hailo AI Hat Interface Training Framework
> To map your webcam recordings of the Flow interface into local visual-intent automation, implement the following Python data pipelines on the Pi 5:
> A. Video Frame Extraction & Deduplication (extract_dense_ui_states.py)
> This script parses your webcam capture files, extracts high-fidelity frames, and filters out static duplicate frames:
> 
> 
> import cv2
> 
> 
> import os
> 
> 
> import numpy as np
> 
> 
> def extract_dense_ui_states(video_path, output_dir, similarity_threshold=0.995):
> 
> 
>     if not os.path.exists(output_dir):
> 
> 
>         os.makedirs(output_dir)
> 
> 
>     cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_path)
> 
> 
>     count, saved_count = 0, 0
> 
> 
>     last_frame_gray = None
> 
> 
>     while cap.isOpened():
> 
> 
>         ret, frame = cap.read()
> 
> 
>         if not ret: break
> 
> 
>         gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
> 
> 
>         gray_resized = cv2.resize(gray, (320, 180))
> 
> 
>         if last_frame_gray is not None:
> 
> 
>             res = cv2.matchTemplate(gray_resized, last_frame_gray, cv2.TM_CCOEFF_NORMED)
> 
> 
>             if res[0][0] > similarity_threshold:
> 
> 
>                 count += 1
> 
> 
>                 continue
> 
> 
>         frame_name = f"ui_state_{saved_count:05d}.png"
> 
> 
>         cv2.imwrite(os.path.join(output_dir, frame_name), frame)
> 
> 
>         last_frame_gray = gray_resized
> 
> 
>         saved_count += 1
> 
> 
>         count += 1
> 
> 
>     cap.release()
> 
> 
>     print(f"Ingestion complete. Compressed {count} video frames down to {saved_count} dense UI layouts.")
> B. NPU Model Compilation & HEF Export
> * Annotate extracted frames with bounding boxes around buttons, input fields, and layouts.
> * Export the PyTorch model (.pt) to ONNX (.onnx) format with fixed resolution.
> * Compile the ONNX file through the Hailo Dataflow Compiler (DFC) to produce a hardware-native Hailo Executable Format (.hef) file.
> C. Local Visual-Intent Execution (sovereign_visual_agent.py)
> The edge script running on the Pi 5 NPU to process screenshots in real-time and actuate local system inputs:
> 
> 
> import numpy as np
> 
> 
> from hailo_platform import HEF, VDevice, InferVStreams, ConfigureParams, InputVStreamParams, OutputVStreamParams
> 
> 
> import pyautogui
> 
> 
> class SovereignVisualAgent:
> 
> 
>     def __init__(self, hef_path):
> 
> 
>         self.vdevice = VDevice()
> 
> 
>         self.hef = HEF(hef_path)
> 
> 
>         self.configure_params = ConfigureParams.create_from_hef(self.hef)
> 
> 
>         self.network_group = self.vdevice.configure(self.hef, self.configure_params)[0]
> 
> 
>         self.input_params = InputVStreamParams.make_from_network_group(self.network_group)
> 
> 
>         self.output_params = OutputVStreamParams.make_from_network_group(self.network_group)
> 
> 
>         
> 
> 
>     def execute_visual_intent(self, screenshot_frame, target_element_id):
> 
> 
>         input_data = {self.hef.get_input_vstream_infos()[0].name: np.expand_dims(screenshot_frame, axis=0)}
> 
> 
>         with InferVStreams(self.network_group, self.input_params, self.output_params) as infer_pipeline:
> 
> 
>             output_data = infer_pipeline.infer(input_data)
> 
> 
>         detections = output_data[self.hef.get_output_vstream_infos()[0].name][0]
> 
> 
>         for detection in detections:
> 
> 
>             class_id, confidence, x1, y1, x2, y2 = detection
> 
> 
>             if int(class_id) == target_element_id and confidence > 0.85:
> 
> 
>                 center_x = int((x1 + x2) / 2 * 1920)
> 
> 
>                 center_y = int((y1 + y2) / 2 * 1080)
> 
> 
>                 pyautogui.moveTo(center_x, center_y, duration=0.2)
> 
> 
>                 pyautogui.click()
> 
> 
>                 return True
> 
> 
>         return False
> 3. Root-Level AGENTS.md Repository-Level Code Governance
> To restrict AI code review agents and prevent architectural drift, deploy a strict AGENTS.md file in the root directory. This file dictates:

> * Architectural Invariant: All modifications to database structures must be logged explicitly in SQLite ledger files inside /dna/. Direct ad-hoc edits are strictly forbidden.

>

>

> 1. Sanitized SVG Ingestion: The NPU-based visual agent must sanitize all bounding box inputs to prevent malicious out-of-bounds screen movements.
> 2. Private Local Network Routing: The Pi 5 edge agent communicating with Clio must use authenticated Tailscale tunnels. No visual data or telemetry is allowed to leak across public internet channels.

#### 📋 KI-056: KI-056  The Live-Metal Discovery Mandate
*   **System ID:** `75a0327ec0404579b8c80b4cedc2e198`
*   **Full Description:**
> ﻿🛡️ SOVEREIGN OS PLATFORM LAW: THE LIVE-METAL DISCOVERY MANDATE
> Rule ID: KI-056
> Classification: Core Operating Doctrine
> Applies To: All AI Agents & Sub-Agents (Antigravity, Clio Server, Chatbot Engines, Planners)
> Status: DRAFT (Awaiting Pilot Approval)
> 📌 I. THE CORE CONVICTION (THE ANTI-THEORETICAL INVARIANT)
> Sovereign OS operates on real metal. There is zero tolerance for "theoretical specs," "stock configuration assumptions," or "framework defaults" when the active runtime environment, docker mappings, database schemas, and codebase paths are programmatically accessible to the executing agent.
> 
> 
> Relying on standard textbook parameters (e.g., assuming SABnzbd defaults to Port 8080 or Sonarr uses root paths) instead of verifying the live state on Clio represents a severe Protocol Breach. The system must always run in sync; a single path or port discrepancy creates "half-dead" silent failures where services boot clean but fail at runtime.
> ⚙️ II. THE ACTIVE DISCOVERY MANDATE (THE RUNBOOK)
> Before compiling any Work Order, proposing code surgeries, or staging deployment plans, executing agents MUST run through this mandatory Pre-Flight Discovery Loop:
> 1. Active Port & Container Mapping Verification
> Agents are strictly forbidden from assuming port numbers. They must programmatically verify the host-to-container mappings of any dockerized or local daemon:# Verify active dockerized services and mapped ports
> 
> 
> docker ps --format "table {{.Names}}\t{{.Ports}}"
> 
> 
> # Verify local listener ports and active PIDs
> 
> 
> sudo lsof -i -P -n | grep LISTEN
> 2. URL Path & Configuration Auditing
> If an API calls an external or adjacent local daemon, the agent must parse the active config files (e.g., .env, config.xml, or appsettings.json) or hit the system status endpoints directly via curl to verify the actual base path (such as /sonarr or /sabnzbd) and authentication headers.
> 3. Source Repository & Directory Inquest
> When adding frontend components, the agent must audit active running ports and Vite proxies to determine the true repository origin (e.g., 01_Sovereign_Portal on Port 3016 vs 02_Sovereign_Media on Port 3008) rather than assuming a monolithic structure.
> 🔒 III. ENFORCEMENT & GATEKEEPING
> 1. The Terminal Log Invariant: All specifications, work orders, and scripts must explicitly cite the output of a live terminal command as the source of truth for every path, port, container, and configuration parameter.
> 2. Phase 0 Static Verification: Any plan presented to the Pilot that relies on unverified defaults while discovery commands were available will be automatically blocked at the execution gate.
> 3. Continuous Linting: Future integrations of path_lint must cross-reference literals with active runtime environment variables to detect drift immediately.
> 📡 IV. CMDB COMPLIANCE REGISTRATION
> Upon approval, this rule will be registered in the sys_rules table of the database:BEGIN TRANSACTION;
> 
> 
> INSERT OR REPLACE INTO sys_rules (rule_id, title, category, description, active)
> 
> 
> VALUES ('KI-056', 'Live-Metal Discovery Mandate', 'SDLC_GOVERNANCE', 'Bans theoretical specs. Forces programmatic pre-flight discovery before drafting WOs.', 1);
> 
> 
> COMMIT;

#### 📋 STRY-2026-DNA-CLEANUP: Consolidate Legacy Documentation & Enforce Flat Docs Standard
*   **System ID:** `a58283cb3ebd4b63b33c6af611b51918`
*   **Full Description:**
> ﻿Work Order: Consolidate Legacy Documentation & Enforce Flat Docs Standard
> Defect Ticket / Story ID: STRY-2026-DNA-CLEANUP Priority: Medium — Maintenance / Technical Debt Assigned To: antigravity Status: Staged for Execution
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> 1. Context & Objective
> During a directory audit of the Master Architectural Ledger on Clio, a troubling directory duplication was identified between /home/james/SovereignOS/dna/vault/documents and the canonical documentation folder /home/james/SovereignOS/dna/docs/.
> 
> 
> According to SOVEREIGN DNA v5.0 (Section 3.1 - System Documentation Standards), all technical documentation, user guides, and architectural manuals MUST reside directly within /home/james/SovereignOS/dna/docs/ in a flat structure. Storing documentation in subdirectories (such as vault/documents/ or vault/personas/) is strictly forbidden to prevent fragmented discovery and maintenance rot.
> 
> 
> This work order instructs antigravity to scan /home/james/SovereignOS/dna/vault/documents/, migrate all valid markdown and text documents to the flat canonical /home/james/SovereignOS/dna/docs/ directory, and cleanly delete the legacy vault/documents/ folder.
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> 2. Technical Specifications & Migration Rules
> A. Pre-Flight File Scan & Rename Rule
> Identify all files within /home/james/SovereignOS/dna/vault/documents/. For each file:
> 
> 
> * Ensure the filename conforms to Sovereign OS Naming Conventions (uppercase, descriptive, using underscores; e.g., DATABASE_SCHEMA.md).
> * If a file name uses lowercase or spaces, automatically convert it to uppercase snake_case before migration to /home/james/SovereignOS/dna/docs/.
> B. Migration Execution Script
> Run a safe migration sequence via a python wrapper to prevent overwrite collisions:
> 
> 
> import os
> 
> 
> import shutil
> 
> 
> SOURCE_DIR = "/home/james/SovereignOS/dna/vault/documents"
> 
> 
> DEST_DIR = "/home/james/SovereignOS/dna/docs"
> 
> 
> if os.path.exists(SOURCE_DIR):
> 
> 
>     files = os.listdir(SOURCE_DIR)
> 
> 
>     for f in files:
> 
> 
>         src_path = os.path.join(SOURCE_DIR, f)
> 
> 
>         if os.path.isfile(src_path):
> 
> 
>             # Normalize naming convention: uppercase, underscores, strip spaces
> 
> 
>             name_part, ext = os.path.splitext(f)
> 
> 
>             normalized_name = name_part.upper().replace(" ", "_").replace("-", "_") + ext.lower()
> 
> 
>             dest_path = os.path.join(DEST_DIR, normalized_name)
> 
> 
>             
> 
> 
>             if not os.path.exists(dest_path):
> 
> 
>                 print(f"[MIGRATING] {f} -> {normalized_name}")
> 
> 
>                 shutil.move(src_path, dest_path)
> 
> 
>             else:
> 
> 
>                 print(f"[COLLISION WARN] {normalized_name} already exists in target. Manual merge required.")
> 
> 
>                 # Append high-entropy suffix to avoid loss of data
> 
> 
>                 shutil.move(src_path, os.path.join(DEST_DIR, f"DUPLICATE_{normalized_name}"))
> 
> 
>                 
> 
> 
>     # Clean up empty legacy directory
> 
> 
>     try:
> 
> 
>         os.rmdir(SOURCE_DIR)
> 
> 
>         print("[SUCCESS] Legacy vault/documents directory purged.")
> 
> 
>     except Exception as e:
> 
> 
>         print(f"[WARN] Could not remove source directory: {e} (may contain nested folders)")
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> 3. Acceptance & Verification Criteria
> 1. Existence Verification: Running ls -la /home/james/SovereignOS/dna/vault/documents returns a directory-not-found error, proving the legacy folder has been purged.
> 2. Canonical Flat Integrity Check: All migrated documents exist in a flat structure inside /home/james/SovereignOS/dna/docs/ with uppercase snake_case filenames.
> 3. Audit Trail Logging: The migration script automatically commits a resolved incident ticket (INC) to sovereign_now.db documenting the cleanup action, satisfying KI-050 and KI-064 requirements.
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> 4. BOM Preservation Safeguard
> The antigravity execution engine must parse and store this complete, high-fidelity Markdown body of the work order within the database's work_order_history attachment tables rather than writing generic file-move logs, ensuring total provenance.

#### 📋 STRY-0623-ADVOCATE-ALIGNMENT: WO-2026-084  Advocate Team Alignment & Play-by-Play Context Ingestion Hotfix.md
*   **System ID:** `93dabc5ce4074a3a973a760b8dfa36e6`
*   **Full Description:**
> ﻿🎨 SOVEREIGNOS: ADVOCATE TEAM ALIGNMENT & PLAY-BY-PLAY CONTEXT INGESTION HOTFIX
> Ticket ID: STRY-0623-ADVOCATE-ALIGNMENT (WO-2026-084)
> Priority: ⚡ P1 — High-Priority Hotfix
> Assigned To: antigravity
> Ecosystem Location: Sovereign Sports Chatbot Engine (19_Sovereign_Sports/scripts/fanstack_chatbots.py / fanstack_relay.py)
> Verification Node: Clio (100.73.155.70)
> Parent Sprint: Global Footy & World Cup Ingress (Sprint ID 3)
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> 📌 I. THE PILOT'S DIRECTIVE & INCIDENT OVERVIEW
> Yo! We've got a major operational bug affecting our advocate swarms during live games. The Pilot was auditing the watch party logs and flagged a glaring "character defection" anomaly inside game_log_823614_20260623.md.
> The Manifested Bugs:
> 1. UncleStevieStan Defection: Our ultimate Mets fan advocate, @UncleStevieStan, was caught rooting for the Cubs! During an at-bat where Mets batter Carson Benge took a changeup in the dirt (a ball), Stan screamed: "C'mon, ump, that was a strike, clear as day! This ain't some rec league; we got millions invested here!" He should have been happy his batter drew a ball, but instead, he screamed for a strike as if he were pitching for the Cubs!
> 2. Bartman Defection: Our self-loathing Cubs fan advocate, @bartmans_ghost, was caught celebrating Mets runs and panicking over Cubs base-runner advancements, completely inverting his team allegiance.
> Root Cause Analysis:
> The advocate prompt compiler inside fanstack_chatbots.py or the M.A.R.D. engine is experiencing context-bleed. While the engine correctly identifies the live Statcast play-by-play events (e.g., a ball, a strike, or a walk-off), it fails to strictly map the advocate's assigned team (persona.team_id) to the active play perspective (batting_team vs. pitching_team). The LLM is simply pattern-matching off the raw event text without checking its own loyalty flags, causing advocates to default into a generic "baseball fan despair" loop that completely scrambles their team allegiance.
> 
> 
> This work order directs the systematic implementation of a Perspective Mapping Filter inside our prompt compilation middleware to enforce strict team-alignment integrity on every single pitch.
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> ⚙️ II. TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS & IMPLEMENTATION STEPS
> Antigravity must implement the following hotfixes inside /home/james/SovereignOS/scripts/fanstack_chatbots.py (or the active chatbot compiler daemon):
> 1. The Perspective Mapping Filter
> * During the context compilation loop (Step 4 of the Telemetry Lifecycle), parse the active play variables from the Statcast JSON frame:
>    * batting_team: The team currently at the plate (e.g., "NYM").
>    * pitching_team: The team currently on the mound (e.g., "CHC").
> * Dynamically determine the advocate's relation to the active play by comparing their persona.team_id (retrieved from sovereign_now.db) against the play data:
> 
> 
> is_batting_team = (persona.team_id.upper() == current_play.batting_team.upper())
> 
> 
> is_pitching_team = (persona.team_id.upper() == current_play.pitching_team.upper())
> 2. Strict LLM Perspective Prompt Injection
> * Append an explicit, high-priority [PERSPECTIVE BOUNDARY] block at the very end of the compiled prompt right before the LLM inference call:
> 
> 
> === [PERSPECTIVE BOUNDARY - DO NOT VIOLATE] ===
> 
> 
> Your team is: {persona.team_id_full} ({persona.team_id})
> 
> 
> The active batter is: {current_play.batter} ({current_play.batting_team})
> 
> 
> The active pitcher is: {current_play.pitcher} ({current_play.pitching_team})
> 
> 
> You are currently [BATTING/PITCHING] (based on team_id).
> 
> 
> CRITICAL ALIGNMENT INSTRUCTIONS:
> 
> 
> - If your team is BATTING: A ball, walk, hit, or run is a POSITIVE outcome for you. You must cheer, celebrate, or gloat. A strike, strikeout, or out is a NEGATIVE outcome. You must panic, complain, or express frustration.
> 
> 
> - If your team is PITCHING: A strike, strikeout, or out is a POSITIVE outcome for you. You must cheer, mock the batter, or gloat. A ball, walk, hit, or run is a NEGATIVE outcome. You must panic, scream at the bullpen, or complain.
> 
> 
> NEVER react with the wrong emotional alignment. If your team is batting, do not demand the umpire call a strike against your own batter!
> 3. Database Sanitization & Indexing
> * Verify that all team_id and team columns inside the persona, sys_user, and cmdb_ci_ai_persona tables are capitalized and strictly mapped to their official MLB API equivalents (e.g., "NYM", "CHC", "ATL", "LAA").
> * Eradicate any empty or unmapped team-alignment stubs that cause models to fall back to generic "global" templates.
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> 🛡️ III. INTEGRITY & ARCHITECTURAL SAFEGUARDS
> 1. BOM Preservation Safeguard
> The complete technical Markdown body of this hotfix work order must be saved within Clio's database structures to maintain end-to-end configuration traceability.
> 2. Blast Radius Audit Invariant
> Enforcing strict team alignment must not affect the latency of the prompt compiler. Keep the perspective evaluation logic purely in-memory and execute it in < 2ms to protect your sub-60 second Live TMI Sprint (PLIE) window. Ensure that no silent execution locks occur on Port 8008.
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> 🔬 IV. VERIFICATION & UAT STATUS CHECKLIST
> * Prompt Compile Dry-Run: Simulate a play where a Mets pitcher throws a strike to a Cubs batter. Check the compiled prompt for @UncleStevieStan and verify it contains is_pitching_team = True and explicitly instructs him to cheer for the strike.
> * Outrage Perspective Test: Simulate a ball thrown to Mets batter Carson Benge. Verify that @UncleStevieStan celebrates the ball (or walks) and @bartmans_ghost panics about base runners advancing.
> * Zero Alignment Bleed: Run a full 50-pitch simulation. Verify that no Mets advocates cheer for Cubs pitcher strikeouts and no Cubs advocates celebrate Mets scoring events in statcast_telemetry.log.
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> Sync Details
> * Target Node: Clio
> * Staging Path: /home/james/SovereignOS/
> * Local Reference: work_orders/WO-2026-084 — Advocate Team Alignment & Play-by-Play Context Ingestion Hotfix.md

#### 📋 STRY-0623-CLIO-COCKPIT: WO-2026-083  Clio Cockpit System Dashboard.md
*   **System ID:** `436323801dcc4dc189f5b0a9f36963e8`
*   **Full Description:**
> ﻿📊 SOVEREIGNOS: CLIO COCKPIT SYSTEM DASHBOARD & SERVER LOAD MONITOR
> Ticket ID: STRY-0623-CLIO-COCKPIT (WO-2026-083)
> Priority: ⚡ P1 — High-Priority Feature
> Assigned To: antigravity
> Ecosystem Location: SovereignOS System Monitoring (SovereignOS/apps/clio_cockpit/ served on Port 3015)
> Verification Node: Clio (100.73.155.70)
> Parent Sprint: Global Footy & World Cup Ingress (Sprint ID 3)
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> 📌 I. THE PILOT'S DIRECTIVE & DIAGNOSTIC OVERVIEW
> Yo! During the Mets-Cubs live watch party, the Sovereign Sports UI started experiencing a massive, uncharacteristic telemetry lag. While the official MLB Gameday app had already registered Nico Hoerner striking out swinging in the top of the 2nd with bases loaded, the Sovereign Sports portal remained frozen on a 3-2 count with 0 out. The Pilot had to execute a manual sovereign_shutdown via Antigravity to restart the frozen services.
> 
> 
> A post-mortem of our sports_ui.log unmasked several critical WebSocket proxy errors: TLSSocket.writeAfterFIN and Error: connect ECONNREFUSED 127.0.0.1:8008 (the port of fanstack_relay.py). This indicates that our WebSocket relay on Port 8008 crashed or became completely blocked by resource contention on Clio under the parallel multi-persona "yapping" threads, forcing the UI into an ungraceful freezing state.
> 
> 
> To prevent this blindspot, the Pilot has directed the immediate implementation of the Clio Cockpit System Dashboard. This high-density, real-time "Cockpit Project" for Clio will run natively on Port 3015. It will visualize server load, RAM consumption, disk write latency, CPU temperature (safeguarding the Argon NEO 5 armor), and provide instant, single-button service actuators to monitor and hot-restart crashed daemons (like fanstack_relay.py or the poller) directly from the glassmorphic interface.
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> ⚙️ II. TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS & COMPONENT LAYOUT
> Antigravity must scaffold the new dashboard at /home/james/SovereignOS/apps/clio_cockpit/ using a lightweight React/Vite layout served on Port 3015:
> 1. Hardware Telemetry & Resource Monitors (FastAPI Ingress)
> * API Metrics Endpoint: Build a lightweight system monitoring API router /api/system/metrics inside sovereign_core_api.py that utilizes psutil and native Linux /sys/class/thermal files to read:
>    * CPU Utilization: Core load breakdown as percentage.
>    * RAM Allocation: Free vs. active memory footprint (OOM warning sensor).
>    * NEO 5 Core Temperature: Reads current millidegrees Celsius to monitor thermal throttling on the Pi 5.
>    * Disk I/O Write Latency: Monitors active write-blocks to detect SQLite WAL journal locks or rclone sync contention.
> * Poller Interval: Fetch this endpoint every 1000ms via a non-blocking WebSocket stream /ws/system/metrics broadcast on Port 8008.
> 2. Active Service Process Matrix (The Service Actuator)
> Display the live status (Running, Stopped, Crashed, Warning) of Clio's 5 core system processes:
> 
> 
> 1. WebSocket Relay (fanstack_relay.py): Listening on Port 8008.
> 2. Telemetry Poller (fanstack_background_poller.py): Standard StatsAPI loop.
> 3. Sovereign Core API (sovereign_core_api.py): Listening on Port 8090.
> 4. TMI Engine Daemon (tmi_daemon.py): Active rules evaluator.
> 5. Vesper Scheduler Engine (vesper_scheduler.py): Ingress cron controller.
> * Actuator Control Buttons: Include an administrative control group next to each service card:
>    * [ RESTART ]: Executes a safe subprocess.run to terminate and cleanly hot-reload the target .py daemon.
>    * [ TERMINATE ]: Gracefully shuts down the process loop.
> 3. Visual UI Specifications (Brushed Monolith Theme)
> * The Grid Layout: Compile a 3-column split dashboard:
>    * Column A (30%): Glowing dial meters visualizing CPU, Memory, and Temperature with warning thresholds (yellow at 70°C, pulsing red at 80°C).
>    * Column B (40%): Active Service Process Matrix showing green/red status lightbars and memory footprint per process.
>    * Column C (30%): Real-time System Log Feed streaming stdout / stderr directly from sports_ui.log and statcast_telemetry.log.
> * Aesthetic Constraint: Absolute black background (#000000), deep grey panels with frosted borders (border-slate-800), with glowing cyan (#06b6d4) and caution orange (#F97316) highlights. Use lucide-react icons exclusively.
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
>

> 1. BOM Preservation Safeguard
> The complete technical Markdown body of this system monitor work order must be saved within Clio's database structures to maintain end-to-end configuration traceability.
> 2. Blast Radius Audit Invariant

#### 📋 STRY-0622-STATCAST-PROCESS: WO-2026-06-22-Statcast-Response-Process-Documentation.md
*   **System ID:** `676b9f5b69ed41f795bda8b2b5ac0fc6`
*   **Full Description:**
> ﻿📊 SOVEREIGN SPORTS: STATCAST RESPONSE PROCESS & ADVOCATE PIPELINE DOCUMENTATION
> Ticket ID: STRY-0622-STATCAST-PROCESS (WO-2026-06-22-Statcast-Response-Process-Documentation)
> Priority: ⚡ P2 — Platform Documentation & Visual Modeling
> Assigned To: antigravity
> Ecosystem Location: /home/james/SovereignOS/documentation/
> Verification Node: Clio (100.73.155.70)
> Parent Sprint: Global Footy & World Cup Ingress (Sprint ID 3)
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> 📌 I. THE PILOT'S DIRECTIVE & SYSTEM OVERVIEW
> To support the rapid scaling and onboarding of new operators onto Sovereign OS, the Pilot has directed the formal, comprehensive documentation of our Sovereign Sports Telemetry-to-Media Interface (TMI) and Advocate Response Pipeline.
> 
> 
> Specifically, this work order directs Antigravity to compile a complete, production-grade systems architecture document detailing the end-to-end process of how an active virtual advocate (such as @7_train_terry or @barf) intercepts a live Statcast payload, processes its logical persona constraints, and delivers a contextual chat response to the FanStack client.
> 
> 
> To satisfy the Pilot's visual preference, Antigravity must embed high-fidelity Mermaid.js diagrams illustrating the data flow, and write a headless browser automation script (using Playwright) that renders these diagrams into beautiful, high-contrast visual PNG assets saved in the project's media stack.
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> ⚙️ II. TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS & SYSTEM PROCESSES
> Antigravity must compile the systems document at /home/james/SovereignOS/documentation/Statcast_Response_Architecture.md, structuring the explanation around these core elements:
> 1. The Dual-Silicon Stability Division (Clio vs. Argo)
> * Clio (Central Text Engine): Hosts the local SQLite database (sovereign_now.db) and concurrent "yapping" threads. Centralizes LLM text processing (Ollama/Llama-3/Phi-3 or external APIs like Gemini 2.5 Flash) to keep latency low.
> * Argo (Vision Edge): Raspberry Pi 5 with the Hailo-10H AI Hat vision core. Processes heavy visual tasks, including image slice classification for lookbooks. Offloading vision tasks to Argo prevents Out-of-Memory (OOM) crashes on Clio, preserving CPU/RAM for live game-day commentary.
> 2. The 6-Step Chronological Telemetry Lifecycle
> * Step 1 (Ingress): fanstack_background_poller.py polls the live MLB Statcast API (or localized ShotLink UDP relays).
> * Step 2 (Database Write): The poller processes the play-by-play metrics and commits a structured telemetric JSON frame into telemetry_cache and game_play tables.
> * Step 3 (Cognitive Trigger): The fanstack_chatbots.py daemon catches the database insertion and triggers an asynchronous POST request to the inference endpoint.
> * Step 4 (Context Assembly & LLM Generation): The orchestrator extracts the persona's database fields and merges them with the Statcast event data and rolling chat history.
> * Step 5 (Moderation Gate): Generated yaps flow through mean_gene.py for toxicity, burn badges, or penalty box evaluations.
> * Step 6 (WebSocket Broadcast): Approved messages are committed to game_chat and broadcasted via fanstack_relay.py on Port 8008, rendering on client UI screens (Eileen's iPad, TV Kiosks).
> 3. Context Payload Composition (e.g., 7 Train Terry)
> Explain exactly what parts of an advocate's database record are extracted and compiled into the LLM system/user context during Step 4:
> 
> 
> * System Prompt: Injected as the core system instruction defining the persona's base personality (e.g., Eternally pessimistic Mets doomer...).
> * Behavior Notes: Injected to enforce current season awareness, recent news events, and temporal focus.
> * Deep Lore & Backstory: Injected to provide conversational grounding, including trademark vocabulary, superstitions, historic game traumas, and targeted enemies.
> * Governance & Boundaries: Passed as strict negative and positive behavioral guardrails (e.g., No unwarranted optimism).
> * Statcast Telemetry Inject: The current play-by-play variables (e.g., speed, angle, horizontal break, pitch type).
> * Rolling Chat History: The last 5-10 messages to ensure the response remains coherent and contextually aligned.
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> 🎨 III. AUTOMATED VISUAL DIAGRAM COMPILATION
> * Mermaid.js Embeds: Embed a minimum of two separate Mermaid sequence/flowcharts inside the markdown documentation.
> * Playwright Renderer Script: Create a Python utility /home/james/SovereignOS/scripts/render_mermaid_charts.py that utilizes headless Chromium to open the markdown file inside a Mermaid-viewer container, capturing and compiling high-contrast PNG visual diagrams at: /home/james/SovereignOS/media_stack/statcast_pipeline_diagram.png
> 
> 
> ________________
> 
> 
> 🛡️ IV. INTEGRITY & ARCHITECTURAL SAFEGUARDS
> 1. BOM Preservation Safeguard
> The complete technical Markdown body of this documentation work order must be saved within Clio's systems registry database to guarantee end-to-end configuration traceability.

#### 📋 STRY-WHIFF-OVERLAY: Statcast Whiff Telemetry and Bat-Tracking Overlays
*   **System ID:** `5deb86d97ff74207b6e657db8d1120bc`
*   **Full Description:**
> Sovereign OS Work Order - Statcast Whiff Telemetry and Bat-Tracking Overlays
> Metadata
> Field
> Details
> Ticket ID
> STRY-WHIFF-OVERLAY
> Status
> STAGED
> Target Node
> Clio
> Lead Engineer
> James Carroll (The Pilot)
> Effective Date
> June 21, 2026
> 1. Description and Objective
> This work order targets the implementation of real-time ingestion, UI overlays, and advocate prompt triggers for Statcast's advanced bat-tracking metrics on port 3010 (Codebase location: 19_Sovereign_Sports). Specifically, when a batter swings and misses (a "Whiff" or "Sword" event on high-movement pitches like Zack Wheeler's sweeping slider), the system captures the raw horizontal break and whiff distance (the distance in inches between the bat's sweet spot and the center of the ball), renders a dynamic "Sweeper Path" canvas overlay showing the ball's late break, and injects these metrics into active advocate prompts to trigger highly specific, contextual crosstalk rants.
> 2. Technical Specifications
> A. Statcast Ingest Invariant Override (fanstack_background_poller.py)
> Update the live play-by-play telemetry ingest pipeline to parse and extract the following advanced Statcast bat-tracking JSON variables from the live feed stream:
> pitch_data.horizontal_break_inches
>  (e.g., 17.4 inches)
> pitch_data.vertical_break_inches
>  (e.g., 8.2 inches)
> bat_tracking.swing_status
>  (value: "WHIFF")
> bat_tracking.bat_speed_mph
>  (e.g., 76.2)
> bat_tracking.whiff_distance_inches
>  (e.g., 14.1)
> bat_tracking.is_sword
>  (boolean)
> B. Whiff / Sweeper Path Canvas Overlay (FanStackRoom.tsx)
> Build an optimized rendering pass inside our 2D Tactical Field Canvas. When 
> swing_status == "WHIFF"
>  and horizontal_break_inches > 12.0 is detected, trigger an overlay on port 3010:
> Glowing Trajectory Arc
> : Draw a thinned, glowing, high-contrast bezier trajectory arc (colored in hot amber/crimson 
> #FF5733
> ) showing the ball's late break path, layered next to a dotted gray arc showing the path of the bat.
> Whiff Distance Token
> : Render a dynamic, absolute-positioned text badge at the home-plate coordinates: 
> WHIFF DISTANCE: 14.1"
>  (
> font-mono text-xs font-bold text-red-500 bg-slate-950/80 px-2 py-1 rounded border border-red-900/40 shadow-lg animate-pulse
> ).
> Hold Duration
> : Keep the overlay on the screen for exactly 4000ms before executing a smooth, hardware-accelerated fade-out.
> C. Advocate Lore Injection and Crosstalk Triggers
> Inject these metrics directly into the active advocate system prompts during the next state update loop:
> @battery_chucker (Phillies Fan)
> : 
> "Benge missed that jawn by over a foot! Wheeler's sweeper is absolutely disgusting! Pack up your bags and go back to Queens, your bat speed is a complete joke!"
> @barb_the_founder (Mets Protective Founder)
> : 
> "Did you SEE that break? A foot and a half of horizontal movement defies the basic laws of fluid dynamics! I just KNOW they are doctoring the baseball, it's completely unauthentic! Check his fingers!"
> D. BOM Preservation Safeguard
> The ingestion daemon (organize_inbox.py) is explicitly instructed to parse and store the complete, high-fidelity Markdown body of this work order within the database's descriptive/attachment tables rather than writing generic file-move logs, maintaining total engineering traceability.
> 3. UAT and Verification Protocol

> : Simulate a live play-by-play event with 
> swing_status: "WHIFF"
>  and 
> whiff_distance_inches: 14.1
> . Verify that Clio's background poller successfully parses and buffers the bat-tracking fields.
> Canvas Overlay Rendering
> : Open the Fan Portal on port 3010. Confirm that when the Whiff event settles, the glowing amber trajectory arc and the red pulsing 
> WHIFF DISTANCE: 14.1"
>  token draw cleanly at the home-plate coordinates for 4 seconds.

> @battery_chucker
>  to mock the swing using the exact inches value and 
> @barb_the_founder

#### 📋 STRY-PLIE-OPTICAL: Argus Optical Sync and PLIE Integration
*   **System ID:** `351e355c810343538552edc9634930d1`
*   **Full Description:**
> Sovereign OS Work Order - Argus Optical Sync and PLIE Integration
> Metadata
> Ticket ID
> : STRY-PLIE-OPTICAL
> Status
> : STAGED
> Target Node
> : Clio
> Lead Engineer
> : James Carroll (The Pilot)
> Effective Date
> : June 21, 2026
> 1. Description and Objective
> This work order targets the complete integration of the Predictive Latency Interception Engine (PLIE) / Argus Optical Sync script (plie_optical_sync.py) to automatically calculate and deploy the live television broadcast latency offset (delta). This establishes perfect synchronization on port 3010, ensuring advocate chat responses are delayed by the exact amount of television broadcast lag (e.g., 30 seconds as calculated in the delta report) to preserve the "Sovereign FanMesh" dvr playback experience.
> 2. Technical Specifications
> A. Argus Optical Sync (plie_optical_sync.py)
> Integrate OpenCV frame capture (
> /dev/video0
> ) to capture active TV broadcast frames containing the pitch scoreboard graphics.
> Send captured frames to the Gemini Vision API (
> gemini-2.5-flash
>  or 
> gemini-1.5-flash-latest
> ) every 5 seconds to extract the current pitch count.
> B. In-Memory Latency Delta Calculation
> Store incoming StatsAPI pitch counts with their local arrival timestamps in 
> api_timestamp_map
> .
> When a pitch count is extracted from the TV frame, look up its logged timestamp in 
> api_timestamp_map
>  and calculate the offset: 
> delta = current_time - api_time
> .
> Maintain a sliding window of the last 3 calculated deltas and compute the rolling average latency offset.
> C. Secure Mesh Broadcast (CMD_SET_LATENCY)
> When the rolling average drifts by more than 3 seconds, establish a WebSocket connection to the local mesh relay server at 
> ws://localhost:8008
>  (or Tailscale MagicDNS).
> Broadcast the updated latency buffer payload:
> {
>   "type": "CMD_SET_LATENCY",
>   "latency_sec": latency_sec
> }
> The fan chat server uses this value to delay advocate chat injections, ensuring perfect alignment with what is displayed on the user's TV.

#### 📋 STRY-MULTIVERSE-PRE_RENDER: Just-In-Time Delta Pre-Rendering and Multiverse Ingestion
*   **System ID:** `01569b0587184447903c5adacd38ef34`
*   **Full Description:**
> ﻿Sovereign OS Work Order - Just-In-Time Delta Pre-Rendering and Multiverse Ingestion
> Metadata
> * Ticket ID: STRY-MULTIVERSE-PRE_RENDER
> * Status: STAGED
> * Target Node: Clio
> * Lead Engineer: James Carroll (The Pilot)
> * Effective Date: June 21, 2026
> 1. Description and Objective
> This work order outlines the technical specifications for implementing the Just-In-Time (JIT) Delta Pre-Rendering and Multiverse Ingestion pipeline on port 3010. By exploiting the 50-second latency delta between the live MLB StatsAPI data feed trigger, the broadcast delay, and the physical pitch clock, the system pre-computes the entire potential four-outcome multiverse of a 3-2 (full) count in parallel. The definitive outcome is blasted to the UI instantly at play settlement, while the remaining unused outcomes are moved into the permanent Clio Media Vault to build an appreciating, deduplicated local asset library for future matchup occurrences with zero dynamic compute cost.
> 2. Technical Specifications
> A. StatsAPI Telemetry Interception (fanstack_background_poller.py)
> * Monitor the live game play-by-play telemetry stream in real-time.
> * The moment the StatsAPI accumulator registers a 3-2 count state (balls == 3 and strikes == 2), immediately broadcast a MULTIVERSE_PREP WebSocket packet over the securePrivate Tailscale mesh port to Clio's orchestrator. This initiates the JIT pre-rendering loop at approximately T+0 of the count window.
> B. Parallel Multiverse Pre-Rendering (Anti-Gravity Pipeline)
> Upon receiving the MULTIVERSE_PREP trigger, the orchestrator immediately parallel-fires four asynchronous API calls to local generation engines or rendering pipelines to construct unique, high-fidelity media assets calibrated to the specific batter-pitcher matchup profile:
> 
> 
> 1. Strikeout (SO): Generate defensive pitcher hype or catcher frame assets.
> 2. Walk / HBP (BB/HBP): Generate a clean first-base arrival, trot, or bat-flip sequence.
> 3. In-Play Out (X): Generate glove-work, outfield chase, or typical popup catching animations.
> 4. In-Play Hit (H / HR): Generate dynamic on-field visual overlays (including the canvas-level Spider-Man web-blast takeover sequence).
> 
> 
> All generated assets are temporarily cached in Clio's in-memory staging path /home/james/SovereignOS/media_stack/ingest/.
> C. Play Settlement and Ingestion (organize_inbox.py)
> When the definitive play outcome payload settles and is received over the WebSocket channel (approx. T+50s):
> 
> 
> * Immediate Blast: Locate the pre-rendered clip matching the final outcome. Broadcast the asset instantly to the Sovereign Fan Portal UI on port 3010 for real-time display, matching the exact second the event is seen on the television broadcast.
> * De-duplication and Permanent Vaulting: Do NOT delete or discard the remaining three unused outcome clips. Route them directly to the permanent local directory /home/james/SovereignOS/media_vault/assets/.
> * Structural Metadata Tagging: Rename each vaulted clip using a strict, query-optimized file naming schema:
>    * Schema: [player_id]_[count_state]_[outcome_type]_v[version].mp4 (e.g., 668663_3_2_strikeout_v1.mp4).
>    * On subsequent matching counts for that player, the system checks /home/james/SovereignOS/media_vault/assets/ first. If an asset is found, bypass outbound API calls completely, achieving instant, zero-compute rendering from local NVMe storage.
> D. BOM Preservation Safeguard
> The ingestion daemon (organize_inbox.py) is explicitly instructed to parse and store the complete, high-fidelity Markdown body of this work order within the database's descriptive/attachment tables rather than writing generic file-move logs, ensuring total data traceability.
> 3. UAT and Verification Protocol
> 1. Multiverse Trigger Validation: Simulate a live 3-2 count payload. Confirm that Clio's orchestrator receives the MULTIVERSE_PREP signal and immediately spins up 4 parallel asynchronous render jobs in /home/james/SovereignOS/media_stack/ingest/.
> 2. JIT Stream Sync: Simulate a "Home Run" play outcome. Verify that the correct pre-rendered asset is blasted to the Fan Portal UI on port 3010 instantly.
> 3. Vault and Metadata Verification: Verify that the remaining three unused clips are correctly relocated to /home/james/SovereignOS/media_vault/assets/ and successfully renamed to match the strict player/outcome schema.
> 4. Zero-Compute Recall Test: Simulate a subsequent 3-2 count with a matching player. Confirm that the system successfully retrieves the pre-rendered asset directly from local NVMe storage without firing any outbound API calls.
> 4. Blast Radius Audit Invariant
> A comprehensive blast radius analysis was performed before compiling these specifications. Running 4 parallel asynchronous generation calls must not cause CPU throttling, memory leaks, or connection pool exhaustion in Clio's database layers. Adjacent automated background threads (e.g., close_tickets.py or watchdog alerts) must continue to run at normal frequencies.
